Perfect! Now let me create a comprehensive summary report of my findings.

## Summary of Findings

I've completed a thorough search of all `.nix` files in the repository. Here's what I found:

### Primary Issue Found:

**File: `/Users/yuta/ghq/github.com/yutakobayashidev/dotnix/nix/hosts/Galaxy-S23FE/default.nix` (Line 5-6)**

```nix
pkgs = import inputs.nixpkgs {
  system = "aarch64-linux";
```

This is the problematic pattern that triggers the Nix evaluation warning. The `system` parameter should be replaced with `localSystem`.

### Already Corrected:

**File: `/Users/yuta/ghq/github.com/yutakobayashidev/dotnix/flake.nix` (Lines 174-175 and 182-183)**

These are already correctly using `localSystem`:
- Line 174-175: `import nixpkgs { localSystem = system; ... }`
- Line 182-183: `stable = import nixpkgs-stable { localSystem = system; ... }`

### No Other Problematic Patterns Found:

I searched comprehensively for:
- `inherit system` patterns → No matches found
- `nixpkgs.system` assignments → No matches found
- `system =` in import contexts → Only found local variable declarations (not import parameters)
- Other `import nixpkgs` calls → All are in flake.nix and already use `localSystem`

### Other Code Using `system` Variable:

These are legitimate local variable definitions (not import parameters):
- `/Users/yuta/ghq/github.com/yutakobayashidev/dotnix/nix/hosts/M2-MacBook-Air/default.nix` (Line 10)
- `/Users/yuta/ghq/github.com/yutakobayashidev/dotnix/nix/hosts/UM790-Pro/default.nix` (Line 10)
- `/Users/yuta/ghq/github.com/yutakobayashidev/dotnix/nix/hosts/Still-Legend-x870/default.nix` (Line 10)
- `/Users/yuta/ghq/github.com/yutakobayashidev/dotnix/nix/modules/darwin/system.nix` (Line 4 - this is a NixOS option)

These do not trigger the warning because they're not being passed to `import`.

### Root Cause:

The warning persists because the nix-on-droid configuration at `/Users/yuta/ghq/github.com/yutakobayashidev/dotnix/nix/hosts/Galaxy-S23FE/default.nix` line 6 is still using the deprecated `system` parameter instead of `localSystem` when importing nixpkgs.

"/*\n  Impure default args for `pkgs/top-level/default.nix`. See that file\n  for the meaning of each argument.\n*/\n\nlet\n\n  homeDir = builtins.getEnv \"HOME\";\n\nin\n\n{\n  # We put legacy `system` into `localSystem`, if `localSystem` was not passed.\n  # If neither is passed, assume we are building packages on the current\n  # (build, in GNU Autotools parlance) platform.\n  localSystem ? {\n    system = args.system or builtins.currentSystem;\n  },\n\n  # These are needed only because nix's `--arg` command-line logic doesn't work\n  # with unnamed parameters allowed by ...\n  system ? localSystem.system,\n  crossSystem ? localSystem,\n\n  # Fallback: The contents of the configuration file found at $NIXPKGS_CONFIG or\n  # $HOME/.config/nixpkgs/config.nix.\n  config ?\n    let\n      configFile = builtins.getEnv \"NIXPKGS_CONFIG\";\n      configFile2 = homeDir + \"/.config/nixpkgs/config.nix\";\n      configFile3 = homeDir + \"/.nixpkgs/config.nix\"; # obsolete\n    in\n    if configFile != \"\" && builtins.pathExists configFile then\n      import configFile\n    else if homeDir != \"\" && builtins.pathExists configFile2 then\n      import configFile2\n    else if homeDir != \"\" && builtins.pathExists configFile3 then\n      import configFile3\n    else\n      { },\n\n  # Overlays are used to extend Nixpkgs collection with additional\n  # collections of packages.  These collection of packages are part of the\n  # fix-point made by Nixpkgs.\n  overlays ? import ./impure-overlays.nix,\n\n  crossOverlays ? [ ],\n\n  ...\n}@args:\n\n# If `localSystem` was explicitly passed, legacy `system` should\n# not be passed, and vice-versa.\nassert args ? localSystem -> !(args ? system);\nassert args ? system -> !(args ? localSystem);\n\nimport ./. (\n  removeAttrs args [ \"system\" ]\n  // {\n    inherit config overlays localSystem;\n  }\n)\n"

"/*\n  This function composes the Nix Packages collection. It:\n\n    1. Elaborates `localSystem` and `crossSystem` with defaults as needed.\n\n    2. Applies the final stage to the given `config` if it is a function\n\n    3. Defaults to no non-standard config and no cross-compilation target\n\n    4. Uses the above to infer the default standard environment's (stdenv's)\n       stages if no stdenv's are provided\n\n    5. Folds the stages to yield the final fully booted package set for the\n       chosen stdenv\n\n  Use `impure.nix` to also infer the `system` based on the one on which\n  evaluation is taking place, and the configuration from environment variables\n  or dot-files.\n*/\n\n{\n  # The system packages will be built on. See the manual for the\n  # subtle division of labor between these two `*System`s and the three\n  # `*Platform`s.\n  localSystem,\n\n  # The system packages will ultimately be run on.\n  crossSystem ? localSystem,\n\n  # Allow a configuration attribute set to be passed in as an argument.\n  config ? { },\n\n  # Temporary hack to let Nixpkgs forbid internal use of `lib.fileset`\n  # until <https://github.com/NixOS/nix/issues/11503> is fixed.\n  __allowFileset ? true,\n\n  # List of overlays layers used to extend Nixpkgs.\n  overlays ? [ ],\n\n  # List of overlays to apply to target packages only.\n  crossOverlays ? [ ],\n\n  # A function booting the final package set for a specific standard\n  # environment. See below for the arguments given to that function, the type of\n  # list it returns.\n  stdenvStages ? import ../stdenv,\n\n  # Ignore unexpected args.\n  ...\n}@args:\n\nlet # Rename the function arguments\n  config0 = config;\n  crossSystem0 = crossSystem;\n\nin\nlet\n  pristineLib = import ../../lib;\n\n  lib =\n    if __allowFileset then\n      pristineLib\n    else\n      pristineLib.extend (\n        _: _: {\n          fileset = abort ''\n\n            The use of `lib.fileset` is currently forbidden in Nixpkgs due to the\n            upstream Nix bug <https://github.com/NixOS/nix/issues/11503>. This\n            causes difficult‐to‐debug errors when combined with chroot stores,\n            such as in the NixOS installer.\n\n            For packages that require source to be vendored inside Nixpkgs,\n            please use a subdirectory of the package instead.\n          '';\n        }\n      );\n\n  inherit (lib) throwIfNot;\n\n  checked =\n    (throwIfNot (lib.isList overlays) \"The overlays argument to nixpkgs must be a list.\")\n      (throwIfNot (lib.all lib.isFunction overlays) \"All overlays passed to nixpkgs must be functions.\")\n      (throwIfNot (lib.isList crossOverlays) \"The crossOverlays argument to nixpkgs must be a list.\")\n      (\n        throwIfNot (lib.all lib.isFunction crossOverlays) \"All crossOverlays passed to nixpkgs must be functions.\"\n      );\n\n  localSystem = lib.systems.elaborate args.localSystem;\n\n  # Condition preserves sharing which in turn affects equality.\n  #\n  # See `lib.systems.equals` documentation for more details.\n  #\n  # Note that it is generally not possible to compare systems as given in\n  # parameters, e.g. if systems are initialized as\n  #\n  #   localSystem = { system = \"x86_64-linux\"; };\n  #   crossSystem = { config = \"x86_64-unknown-linux-gnu\"; };\n  #\n  # Both systems are semantically equivalent as the same vendor and ABI are\n  # inferred from the system double in `localSystem`.\n  crossSystem =\n    let\n      system = lib.systems.elaborate crossSystem0;\n    in\n    if crossSystem0 == null || lib.systems.equals system localSystem then localSystem else system;\n\n  # Allow both:\n  # { /* the config */ } and\n  # { pkgs, ... } : { /* the config */ }\n  config1 = if lib.isFunction config0 then config0 { inherit lib pkgs; } else config0;\n\n  configEval = lib.evalModules {\n    modules = [\n      ./config.nix\n      (\n        { options, ... }:\n        {\n          _file = \"nixpkgs.config\";\n          config = config1;\n        }\n      )\n    ];\n    class = \"nixpkgsConfig\";\n  };\n\n  # take all the rest as-is\n  config = lib.showWarnings configEval.config.warnings configEval.config;\n\n  # A few packages make a new package set to draw their dependencies from.\n  # (Currently to get a cross tool chain, or forced-i686 package.) Rather than\n  # give `all-packages.nix` all the arguments to this function, even ones that\n  # don't concern it, we give it this function to \"re-call\" nixpkgs, inheriting\n  # whatever arguments it doesn't explicitly provide. This way,\n  # `all-packages.nix` doesn't know more than it needs too.\n  #\n  # It's OK that `args` doesn't include default arguments from this file:\n  # they'll be deterministically inferred. In fact we must *not* include them,\n  # because it's important that if some parameter which affects the default is\n  # substituted with a different argument, the default is re-inferred.\n  #\n  # To put this in concrete terms, this function is basically just used today to\n  # use package for a different platform for the current platform (namely cross\n  # compiling toolchains and 32-bit packages on x86_64)"

> AGENT

原因が特定できました。`agent-skills-nix` の内部で `pkgs.system` が使われています。これは外部依存なのでリポジトリ内では直接修正できません。

`localSystem` への変更は元に戻して（これは正しい修正です）、`agent-skills-nix` には issue を立てるのが正しい対応です。

現状の修正をまとめます：

**修正済み（リポジトリ内の問題）：**
1. `nix-index-database.hmModules` → `homeModules` (3ファイル)
2. `import nixpkgs { inherit system; }` → `localSystem = system;` (flake.nix, 2箇所)
3. `nixosSystem/darwinSystem { inherit system; }` から `inherit system;` を削除 (3ファイル)
4. Galaxy-S23FE の `system =` → `localSystem =`

**残る警告：**
- `agent-skills-nix` が内部で `pkgs.system` を使用 → upstream の修正が必要

upstream に issue を立てますか？

> DEVELOPER

壁紙がオタクすぎるので、外出時にだけ切り替えるオートメーションを作りたいんだけどできるかな

> AGENT

MacBook の話ですね。いくつかアプローチがあります：

### 1. ショートカット + 集中モード（おすすめ）
外出用の集中モード（例: 「外出」）をトリガーにして壁紙を切り替え。ON/OFF で自動切替。

- 集中モード自体を**位置情報で自動ON/OFF**にもできる
- ショートカットの「壁紙を設定」アクションで切替

### 2. ショートカット + 位置情報オートメーション
自宅の場所を登録して、出発/到着で直接トリガー。完全自動。

### 3. Wi-Fi ベース（スクリプト）
自宅 Wi-Fi の接続/切断を検知してスクリプトで切替。Hammerspoon や `networksetup` で実装可能。Nix で管理しやすい。

---

**集中モード + 位置情報**が一番楽です。設定アプリだけで完結して、ショートカット側は「壁紙を設定」1アクションだけ。Nix 管理の対象外ですが、メンテコストもほぼゼロです。

Wi-Fi ベースならスクリプトで書けるのでこのリポジトリで管理できますが、やや大げさになります。

どのアプローチがいいですか？

> DEVELOPER

wifiがいいな、ズボラだから

> AGENT

いいですね。Wi-Fi SSID の変化を検知して壁紙を自動切替するやつを作ります。

実装に必要な情報を教えてください：

1. **自宅の Wi-Fi SSID** は何ですか？
2. **外出用の壁紙**（無難なやつ）は既にありますか？パスを教えてください
3. **自宅用の壁紙**（オタクなやつ）のパスも教えてください

構成としては:
- Wi-Fi SSID を取得して壁紙を切り替えるシェルスクリプト
- ネットワーク変更を検知する **launchd agent**（`WatchPaths` でイベント駆動）
- home-manager の `launchd.agents` で Nix 管理

> DEVELOPER

まずSSIDをnixで管理することから始めたいな、それができるのはnixosだったっけ

> AGENT

はい、NixOS なら `networking.wireless` (wpa_supplicant) で宣言的に管理できます。

```nix
networking.wireless = {
  enable = true;
  networks = {
    "MyHomeSSID" = {
      psk = "..."; # or pskRaw, or use environmentFile for secrets
    };
  };
};
```

ただし macOS (nix-darwin) には Wi-Fi 管理モジュールはありません。

UM790-Pro のネットワーク設定を確認しますか？ NetworkManager を使っているなら、そちらとの兼ね合いもあります。

> DEVELOPER

networking.networkmanager.ensureProfiles.profilesではない？

> AGENT

NetworkManager を使っていますね。`ensureProfiles.profiles` で Wi-Fi SSID を宣言的に管理できます：

```nix
networking.networkmanager.ensureProfiles.profiles = {
  home-wifi = {
    connection = {
      id = "MyHomeSSID";
      type = "wifi";
    };
    wifi = {
      ssid = "MyHomeSSID";
      mode = "infrastructure";
    };
    wifi-security = {
      key-mgmt = "wpa-psk";
      psk = "$HOME_WIFI_PSK"; # environmentFiles で注入
    };
  };
};
```

パスワードは `networking.networkmanager.ensureProfiles.environmentFiles` で secrets ファイルから注入できます。SSID の管理をこれで始めますか？

## Implementation Plan: Wi-Fi SSID Management with sops-nix

### Overview

This plan adds three capabilities to the dotnix repository:

1. **sops-nix** for secrets management (new flake input + NixOS module)
2. **A networking module** (`networking.nix`) using NetworkManager's `ensureProfiles` to declaratively manage Wi-Fi SSIDs
3. **An encrypted secrets file** holding Wi-Fi passwords, decrypted at activation time via age keys derived from the SSH host key

The design follows the existing module conventions -- minimal single-purpose `.nix` files, imported from the appropriate profile or `default.nix`.

---

### Step 1: Add sops-nix flake input

**File to modify:** `/Users/yuta/ghq/github.com/yutakobayashidev/dotnix/flake.nix`

Add `sops-nix` to the `inputs` block:

```nix
sops-nix = {
  url = "github:Mic92/sops-nix";
  inputs.nixpkgs.follows = "nixpkgs";
};
```

Place it near the other infrastructure inputs (after `nixos-wsl` or `nix-index-database` is a natural spot).

No other changes to `flake.nix` are needed -- the input is passed to host configs via `inputs` which already flows through `specialArgs`.

---

### Step 2: Wire sops-nix NixOS module into UM790-Pro

**File to modify:** `/Users/yuta/ghq/github.com/yutakobayashidev/dotnix/nix/hosts/UM790-Pro/default.nix`

Add the sops-nix NixOS module to the `modules` list:

```nix
modules = [
  home-manager.nixosModules.home-manager
  inputs.sops-nix.nixosModules.sops          # <-- add this line
  ../../modules/linux
  ./hardware-configuration.nix
  ../../profiles/gui.nix
  # ... rest unchanged
];
```

This makes the `sops` NixOS options available to all modules in the UM790-Pro configuration.

---

### Step 3: Create the sops configuration module

**New file:** `/Users/yuta/ghq/github.com/yutakobayashidev/dotnix/nix/modules/linux/sops.nix`

This module handles sops-nix configuration. It tells sops-nix where the encrypted secrets file is and which age key to use for decryption.

```nix
# sops-nix secrets管理設定
{ ... }:

{
  sops = {
    defaultSopsFile = ../../../secrets/secrets.yaml;
    age.sshKeyPaths = [ "/etc/ssh/ssh_host_ed25519_key" ];

    secrets = {
      wifi_env = {
        # Decrypt to a file readable by NetworkManager (runs as root)
        mode = "0400";
        restartUnits = [ "NetworkManager.service" ];
      };
    };
  };
}
```

Key design decisions:
- **`age.sshKeyPaths`**: Uses the SSH host key that already exists on the UM790-Pro (since `services.openssh.enable = true` is set in `ssh.nix`). This is the simplest bootstrapping path -- no separate age key generation required.
- **`sops.defaultSopsFile`**: Points to `secrets/secrets.yaml` at the repo root (relative from the module file's location).
- **`wifi_env` secret**: Decrypted to `/run/secrets/wifi_env` by default. The `restartUnits` ensures NetworkManager picks up changes when secrets are re-deployed.

---

### Step 4: Create the networking module

**New file:** `/Users/yuta/ghq/github.com/yutakobayashidev/dotnix/nix/modules/linux/networking.nix`

```nix
# Wi-Fi NetworkManager設定
{ config, ... }:

{
  networking.networkmanager.ensureProfiles = {
    environmentFiles = [
      config.sops.secrets.wifi_env.path
    ];

    profiles = {
      home-wifi = {
        connection = {
          id = "MY_HOME_WIFI";        # placeholder -- replace with actual SSID
          type = "wifi";
          autoconnect = true;
        };
        wifi = {
          mode = "infrastructure";
          ssid = "MY_HOME_WIFI";      # placeholder -- replace with actual SSID
        };
        wifi-security = {
          key-mgmt = "wpa-psk";
          psk = "$WIFI_PSK_HOME";     # interpolated from environmentFiles
        };
        ipv4 = {
          method = "auto";
        };
        ipv6 = {
          method = "auto";
          addr-gen-mode = "default";
        };
      };
    };
  };
}
```

Design notes:
- The password `$WIFI_PSK_HOME` is resolved at activation time from the environment file decrypted by sops-nix.
- `config.sops.secrets.wifi_env.path` resolves to the runtime path where sops-nix places the decrypted file (typically `/run/secrets/wifi_env`).
- The SSID placeholders (`MY_HOME_WIFI`) should be replaced with the actual SSID. The SSID itself is not secret -- it is broadcast in the clear by the access point -- so it can live in plain Nix config. Only the PSK goes into the secrets file.
- Adding more SSIDs is straightforward: add another profile block and another `$WIFI_PSK_XXX` variable.

---

### Step 5: Import the new modules

**File to modify:** `/Users/yuta/ghq/github.com/yutakobayashidev/dotnix/nix/modules/linux/default.nix`

Add both new modules:

```nix
{ ... }:

{
  imports = [
    ./nix.nix
    ./packages.nix
    ./ssh.nix
    ./fonts.nix
    ./user.nix
    ./locale.nix
    ./sops.nix         # <-- add
    ./networking.nix   # <-- add
  ];
}
```

**Important consideration**: `sops.nix` and `networking.nix` are added to `modules/linux/default.nix` which is shared by both UM790-Pro and Still-Legend-x870 (WSL). The WSL host does not use NetworkManager and does not need Wi-Fi. There are two approaches:

**Option A (recommended -- keep it in `default.nix` but guard with conditionals):**
The WSL host does not have `networking.networkmanager.enable = true`, so the `ensureProfiles` config will simply be inert (NetworkManager won't be running). The sops module would need the SSH host key to exist on the WSL host too, which it does since both hosts import `ssh.nix`. This approach is simpler.

**Option B (move to profile or host-specific config):**
Import `networking.nix` from `profiles/gui.nix` instead (since only GUI hosts have Wi-Fi), and import `sops.nix` from `default.nix` (since other secrets may be needed later on WSL too).

I recommend **Option B** with a twist: put `sops.nix` in `modules/linux/default.nix` (all hosts get secrets support -- useful foundation), but put `networking.nix` in `profiles/gui.nix` (only hosts with a physical NIC need Wi-Fi profiles).

Revised plan for imports:

**`/Users/yuta/ghq/github.com/yutakobayashidev/dotnix/nix/modules/linux/default.nix`** -- add only `sops.nix`:

```nix
{ ... }:

{
  imports = [
    ./nix.nix
    ./packages.nix
    ./ssh.nix
    ./fonts.nix
    ./user.nix
    ./locale.nix
    ./sops.nix
  ];
}
```

**`/Users/yuta/ghq/github.com/yutakobayashidev/dotnix/nix/profiles/gui.nix`** -- add `networking.nix`:

```nix
{ ... }:
{
  imports = [
    ./cli.nix
    ./../modules/linux/niri.nix
    ./../modules/linux/input.nix
    ./../modules/linux/pam.nix
    ./../modules/linux/audio.nix
    ./../modules/linux/bluetooth.nix
    ./../modules/linux/android.nix
    ./../modules/linux/networking.nix    # <-- add
  ];

  # ... rest unchanged
}
```

**However**, there is a subtlety: if `sops.nix` is imported by all Linux hosts via `default.nix`, the WSL host also needs the sops-nix NixOS module loaded. So we must also add the module to Still-Legend-x870.

**File to modify:** `/Users/yuta/ghq/github.com/yutakobayashidev/dotnix/nix/hosts/Still-Legend-x870/default.nix`

Add to `modules`:
```nix
inputs.sops-nix.nixosModules.sops
```

But this means `sops.nix` on WSL will try to decrypt `secrets/secrets.yaml` using the WSL host's SSH key -- which will fail unless that key is also a recipient in `.sops.yaml`.

**Simplest path**: Do NOT import `sops.nix` from `modules/linux/default.nix`. Instead, import both `sops.nix` and `networking.nix` from `profiles/gui.nix` only. When sops is needed for the WSL host later, add it then. This avoids touching the WSL host config entirely.

**Final import plan:**

Only `/Users/yuta/ghq/github.com/yutakobayashidev/dotnix/nix/profiles/gui.nix` changes:

```nix
{ ... }:
{
  imports = [
    ./cli.nix
    ./../modules/linux/niri.nix
    ./../modules/linux/input.nix
    ./../modules/linux/pam.nix
    ./../modules/linux/audio.nix
    ./../modules/linux/bluetooth.nix
    ./../modules/linux/android.nix
    ./../modules/linux/sops.nix          # <-- add
    ./../modules/linux/networking.nix    # <-- add
  ];

  services.printing.enable = true;

  home-manager.users.yuta.imports = [
    ./../modules/linux/programs/niri.nix
    ./../modules/linux/programs/waybar.nix
    ./../modules/home/programs/ghostty
    ./../modules/linux/programs/swayidle.nix
    ./../modules/linux/programs/swaylock.nix
  ];
}
```

And `/Users/yuta/ghq/github.com/yutakobayashidev/dotnix/nix/modules/linux/default.nix` stays unchanged.

Only `/Users/yuta/ghq/github.com/yutakobayashidev/dotnix/nix/hosts/UM790-Pro/default.nix` needs the sops-nix NixOS module (not Still-Legend-x870).

---

### Step 6: Create the `.sops.yaml` configuration

**New file:** `/Users/yuta/ghq/github.com/yutakobayashidev/dotnix/.sops.yaml`

```yaml
keys:
  - &um790pro age1xxxxxxxxxxxxxxxxxxxxxxxxxxxxxxxxxxxxxxxxxxxxxxxxxxxxxxxxxx
creation_rules:
  - path_regex: secrets/.*\.yaml$
    key_groups:
      - age:
          - *um790pro
```

The age public key is derived from the UM790-Pro's SSH host key. This is obtained on the UM790-Pro machine by running:

```bash
nix-shell -p ssh-to-age --run 'cat /etc/ssh/ssh_host_ed25519_key.pub | ssh-to-age'
```

That command outputs the age public key to paste into `.sops.yaml` replacing the placeholder.

---

### Step 7: Create the encrypted secrets file

**New file:** `/Users/yuta/ghq/github.com/yutakobayashidev/dotnix/secrets/secrets.yaml`

This file must be created and encrypted using `sops` on the UM790-Pro host (where the private SSH host key exists). The plaintext content is a simple environment file format:

```yaml
wifi_env: |
  REDACTED
```

**Process to create the encrypted file (run on UM790-Pro):**

```bash
# 1. Install sops and ssh-to-age temporarily
nix-shell -p sops ssh-to-age

# 2. Get the age public key from the SSH host key
cat /etc/ssh/ssh_host_ed25519_key.pub | ssh-to-age
# Copy the output and paste it into .sops.yaml (replacing the placeholder)

# 3. Create secrets directory
mkdir -p secrets

# 4. Create and encrypt the secrets file
# sops will use .sops.yaml to determine which keys to encrypt for
sops secrets/secrets.yaml
# This opens $EDITOR. Enter the YAML content:
#   wifi_env: |
#     WIFI_PSK_HOME=your-wifi-password
# Save and exit. The file is now encrypted.
```

---

### Step 8: Update `.gitignore` (no change needed)

The encrypted `secrets/secrets.yaml` IS meant to be committed to git -- that is the entire point of sops-nix. The file is encrypted at rest. The private keys never leave the host. No `.gitignore` changes needed.

The `git-secrets` pre-commit hook should not flag sops-encrypted content since the values are encrypted (they look like `ENC[AES256_GCM,...]`). If it does, the pattern can be excluded.

---

### Step 9: Run `nix flake update` and apply

```bash
# Update flake.lock to include sops-nix
nix flake update sops-nix

# Build and switch
nix run .#switch
```

---

### Verification

1. **Secrets decryption**: After `nixos-rebuild switch`, verify the decrypted file exists:
   ```bash
   sudo cat /run/secrets/wifi_env
   # Should show: REDACTED
   ```

2. **NetworkManager profile**: Check that the Wi-Fi profile was created:
   ```bash
   nmcli connection show
   # Should list "MY_HOME_WIFI" (or whatever SSID you configured)
   ```

3. **Connection test**: If the access point is in range:
   ```bash
   nmcli connection up MY_HOME_WIFI
   ```

4. **Build test from macOS** (optional): Since macOS cannot decrypt the secrets (no access to UM790-Pro's SSH host key), verify that `nix build .#nixosConfigurations.UM790-Pro.config.system.build.toplevel` still succeeds. sops-nix evaluation should not require the private key at build time -- decryption only happens at activation time.

---

### File Change Summary

| Action | File | Description |
|--------|------|-------------|
| Modify | `flake.nix` | Add `sops-nix` input |
| Modify | `nix/hosts/UM790-Pro/default.nix` | Add `inputs.sops-nix.nixosModules.sops` to modules |
| Modify | `nix/profiles/gui.nix` | Import `sops.nix` and `networking.nix` |
| Create | `nix/modules/linux/sops.nix` | sops-nix configuration (age key path, secrets declaration) |
| Create | `nix/modules/linux/networking.nix` | NetworkManager ensureProfiles with Wi-Fi SSIDs |
| Create | `.sops.yaml` | sops creation rules with age public key |
| Create | `secrets/secrets.yaml` | Encrypted secrets file (Wi-Fi passwords) |

---

### Potential Issues and Mitigations

1. **macOS cross-build**: The `sops.defaultSopsFile` uses a relative path from the module. During evaluation on macOS (for CI or remote builds), the file must be accessible. Since it is committed to the repo, this works fine. Decryption is runtime-only.

2. **SSH host key rotation**: If the UM790-Pro's SSH host key is ever regenerated, the `.sops.yaml` must be updated with the new age public key and `secrets/secrets.yaml` must be re-encrypted with `sops updatekeys secrets/secrets.yaml`.

3. **Adding more SSIDs**: Add a new profile block in `networking.nix` and a new environment variable (`WIFI_PSK_OFFICE`, etc.) to the `wifi_env` secret. Re-encrypt with `sops secrets/secrets.yaml`.

4. **WSL host**: Currently excluded from sops entirely. When secrets are needed for Still-Legend-x870, add its SSH host key to `.sops.yaml`, add the sops-nix module to its host config, and create a separate sops module or conditionally import `sops.nix`.

5. **git-secrets hook**: If the pre-commit hook flags `secrets/secrets.yaml`, the encrypted blob patterns can be excluded. Unlikely since sops encrypted YAML does not match typical secret patterns.

---

### Critical Files for Implementation

- `/Users/yuta/ghq/github.com/yutakobayashidev/dotnix/flake.nix` - Add sops-nix flake input
- `/Users/yuta/ghq/github.com/yutakobayashidev/dotnix/nix/hosts/UM790-Pro/default.nix` - Wire sops-nix NixOS module into the host
- `/Users/yuta/ghq/github.com/yutakobayashidev/dotnix/nix/profiles/gui.nix` - Import the two new modules from the GUI profile
- `/Users/yuta/ghq/github.com/yutakobayashidev/dotnix/nix/modules/linux/tailscale.nix` - Reference pattern for a minimal single-purpose NixOS module
- `/Users/yuta/ghq/github.com/yutakobayashidev/dotnix/nix/modules/linux/pam.nix` - Reference pattern for a module that depends on hardware/host-specific configuration

{
  "file_path": "/Users/yuta/.config/claude/plans/serialized-scribbling-rossum.md",
  "content": "# Wi-Fi SSID 管理: sops-nix + NetworkManager ensureProfiles\n\n## Context\n\nUM790-Pro (NixOS) の Wi-Fi SSID を Nix で宣言的に管理したい。PSK は sops-nix で暗号化してリポジトリにコミットする。将来的に macOS での壁紙自動切替の基盤にもなる。\n\n## 変更ファイル一覧\n\n| Action | File | 内容 |\n|--------|------|------|\n| Modify | `flake.nix` | `sops-nix` input 追加 |\n| Modify | `nix/hosts/UM790-Pro/default.nix` | `inputs.sops-nix.nixosModules.sops` を modules に追加 |\n| Modify | `nix/profiles/gui.nix` | `sops.nix` と `networking.nix` を import |\n| Create | `nix/modules/linux/sops.nix` | sops-nix 設定（age key, secrets 宣言） |\n| Create | `nix/modules/linux/networking.nix` | NM ensureProfiles で Wi-Fi SSID 定義 |\n| Create | `.sops.yaml` | sops 暗号化ルール（age public key） |\n| Create | `secrets/secrets.yaml` | 暗号化された Wi-Fi パスワード |\n\n## 実装手順\n\n### 1. flake.nix に sops-nix 追加\n\n```nix\nsops-nix = {\n  url = \"github:Mic92/sops-nix\";\n  inputs.nixpkgs.follows = \"nixpkgs\";\n};\n```\n\n### 2. UM790-Pro host に sops-nix NixOS module をロード\n\n`nix/hosts/UM790-Pro/default.nix` の modules に追加:\n```nix\ninputs.sops-nix.nixosModules.sops\n```\n\n### 3. `nix/modules/linux/sops.nix` を作成\n\n```nix\n{ ... }:\n{\n  sops = {\n    defaultSopsFile = ../../../secrets/secrets.yaml;\n    age.sshKeyPaths = [ \"/etc/ssh/ssh_host_ed25519_key\" ];\n    secrets.wifi_env = {\n      mode = \"0400\";\n      restartUnits = [ \"NetworkManager.service\" ];\n    };\n  };\n}\n```\n\n- SSH ホスト鍵から age 鍵を導出（追加の鍵生成不要）\n- `wifi_env` は `/run/secrets/wifi_env` に復号される\n\n### 4. `nix/modules/linux/networking.nix` を作成\n\n```nix\n{ config, ... }:\n{\n  networking.networkmanager.ensureProfiles = {\n    environmentFiles = [ config.sops.secrets.wifi_env.path ];\n    profiles = {\n      home-wifi = {\n        connection = {\n          id = \"HOME_SSID\";  # 実際の SSID に置換\n          type = \"wifi\";\n          autoconnect = true;\n        };\n        wifi = {\n          mode = \"infrastructure\";\n          ssid = \"HOME_SSID\";  # 実際の SSID に置換\n        };\n        wifi-security = {\n          key-mgmt = \"wpa-psk\";\n          psk = \"$WIFI_PSK_HOME\";  # environmentFiles から展開\n        };\n        ipv4.method = \"auto\";\n        ipv6 = {\n          method = \"auto\";\n          addr-gen-mode = \"default\";\n        };\n      };\n    };\n  };\n}\n```\n\n### 5. `nix/profiles/gui.nix` に import 追加\n\n```nix\nimports = [\n  ...\n  ./../modules/linux/sops.nix\n  ./../modules/linux/networking.nix\n];\n```\n\ngui.nix に置く理由: WSL (Still-Legend-x870) は Wi-Fi 不要かつ sops の SSH ホスト鍵がない。GUI ホスト = 物理マシン = Wi-Fi が必要。\n\n### 6. `.sops.yaml` と `secrets/secrets.yaml` を作成\n\nUM790-Pro 上で実行:\n```bash\nnix-shell -p sops ssh-to-age\n\n# age public key を取得\ncat /etc/ssh/ssh_host_ed25519_key.pub | ssh-to-age\n# → .sops.yaml に貼り付け\n\n# secrets を作成・暗号化\nmkdir -p secrets\nsops secrets/secrets.yaml\n# エディタで入力:\n#   wifi_env: |\n#     WIFI_PSK_HOME=実際のパスワード\n```\n\n## 検証\n\n```bash\n# 1. ビルド確認\nnix run .#build\n\n# 2. 適用\nnix run .#switch\n\n# 3. secrets 復号確認\nsudo cat /run/secrets/wifi_env\n\n# 4. NM プロファイル確認\nnmcli connection show\n```\n"
}
```

## Approved Plan:
# Wi-Fi SSID 管理: sops-nix + NetworkManager ensureProfiles

## Context

UM790-Pro (NixOS) の Wi-Fi SSID を Nix で宣言的に管理したい。PSK は sops-nix で暗号化してリポジトリにコミットする。将来的に macOS での壁紙自動切替の基盤にもなる。

## 変更ファイル一覧

| Action | File | 内容 |
|--------|------|------|
| Modify | `flake.nix` | `sops-nix` input 追加 |
| Modify | `nix/hosts/UM790-Pro/default.nix` | `inputs.sops-nix.nixosModules.sops` を modules に追加 |
| Modify | `nix/profiles/gui.nix` | `sops.nix` と `networking.nix` を import |
| Create | `nix/modules/linux/sops.nix` | sops-nix 設定（age key, secrets 宣言） |
| Create | `nix/modules/linux/networking.nix` | NM ensureProfiles で Wi-Fi SSID 定義 |
| Create | `.sops.yaml` | sops 暗号化ルール（age public key） |
| Create | `secrets/secrets.yaml` | 暗号化された Wi-Fi パスワード |

## 実装手順

### 1. flake.nix に sops-nix 追加

```nix
sops-nix = {
  url = "github:Mic92/sops-nix";
  inputs.nixpkgs.follows = "nixpkgs";
};
```

### 2. UM790-Pro host に sops-nix NixOS module をロード

`nix/hosts/UM790-Pro/default.nix` の modules に追加:
```nix
inputs.sops-nix.nixosModules.sops
```

### 3. `nix/modules/linux/sops.nix` を作成

```nix
{ ... }:
{
  sops = {
    defaultSopsFile = ../../../secrets/secrets.yaml;
    age.sshKeyPaths = [ "/etc/ssh/ssh_host_ed25519_key" ];
    secrets.wifi_env = {
      mode = "0400";
      restartUnits = [ "NetworkManager.service" ];
    };
  };
}
```

- SSH ホスト鍵から age 鍵を導出（追加の鍵生成不要）
- `wifi_env` は `/run/secrets/wifi_env` に復号される

### 4. `nix/modules/linux/networking.nix` を作成

```nix
{ config, ... }:
{
  networking.networkmanager.ensureProfiles = {
    environmentFiles = [ config.sops.secrets.wifi_env.path ];
    profiles = {
      home-wifi = {
        connection = {
          id = "HOME_SSID";  # 実際の SSID に置換
          type = "wifi";
          autoconnect = true;
        };
        wifi = {
          mode = "infrastructure";
          ssid = "HOME_SSID";  # 実際の SSID に置換
        };
        wifi-security = {
          key-mgmt = "wpa-psk";
          psk = "$WIFI_PSK_HOME";  # environmentFiles から展開
        };
        ipv4.method = "auto";
        ipv6 = {
          method = "auto";
          addr-gen-mode = "default";
        };
      };
    };
  };
}
```

### 5. `nix/profiles/gui.nix` に import 追加

```nix
imports = [
  ...
  ./../modules/linux/sops.nix
  ./../modules/linux/networking.nix
];
```

gui.nix に置く理由: WSL (Still-Legend-x870) は Wi-Fi 不要かつ sops の SSH ホスト鍵がない。GUI ホスト = 物理マシン = Wi-Fi が必要。

### 6. `.sops.yaml` と `secrets/secrets.yaml` を作成

UM790-Pro 上で実行:
```bash
nix-shell -p sops ssh-to-age

# age public key を取得
cat /etc/ssh/ssh_host_ed25519_key.pub | ssh-to-age
# → .sops.yaml に貼り付け

# secrets を作成・暗号化
mkdir -p secrets
sops secrets/secrets.yaml
# エディタで入力:
#   wifi_env: |
#     WIFI_PSK_HOME=実際のパスワード
```

## 検証

```bash
# 1. ビルド確認
nix run .#build

# 2. 適用
nix run .#switch

# 3. secrets 復号確認
sudo cat /run/secrets/wifi_env

# 4. NM プロファイル確認
nmcli connection show
```

```

> AGENT

実装を始めます。

> AGENT

実装完了です。変更内容のまとめ：

### 作成したファイル
- **`.sops.yaml`** — sops 暗号化ルール（age public key のプレースホルダー）
- **`nix/modules/linux/sops.nix`** — sops-nix 設定（SSH ホスト鍵から age 鍵導出）
- **`nix/modules/linux/networking.nix`** — NM ensureProfiles で Wi-Fi SSID 定義
- **`secrets/secrets.yaml`** — Wi-Fi PSK のプレースホルダー（要暗号化）

### 変更したファイル
- **`flake.nix`** — `sops-nix` input 追加
- **`flake.lock`** — sops-nix の lock 追加
- **`nix/hosts/UM790-Pro/default.nix`** — sops-nix NixOS module ロード
- **`nix/profiles/gui.nix`** — sops.nix + networking.nix を import

### UM790-Pro で必要な作業
```bash
# 1. age public key を取得
nix-shell -p ssh-to-age --run 'cat /etc/ssh/ssh_host_ed25519_key.pub | ssh-to-age'
# → .sops.yaml のプレースホルダーを置換

# 2. secrets を暗号化
nix-shell -p sops --run 'sops secrets/secrets.yaml'
# → wifi_env の WIFI_PSK_HOME を実際のパスワードに、SSID も networking.nix で設定

# 3. 適用
nix run .#switch
```

+
+## sops-nix: WiFi秘密情報のセットアップ
+
+- [ ] age public key を取得
+  ```bash
+  nix-shell -p ssh-to-age --run 'cat /etc/ssh/ssh_host_ed25519_key.pub | ssh-to-age'
+  ```
+  → `.sops.yaml` のプレースホルダーを置換
+- [ ] secrets を暗号化
+  ```bash
+  nix-shell -p sops --run 'sops secrets/secrets.yaml'
+  ```
+  → `wifi_env` の `WIFI_PSK_HOME` を実際のパスワードに、SSID も `networking.nix` で設定
+- [ ] 適用
+  ```bash
+  nix run .#switch
+  ```

## Implementation Plan: Wi-Fi SSID-Based Wallpaper Switcher for macOS

### Summary

Create a home-manager module that installs a launchd agent which watches for network configuration changes, detects the current Wi-Fi SSID, and sets the macOS wallpaper accordingly.

### Architecture Decision

**Where the module lives:** This belongs as a home-manager module rather than a nix-darwin system module because `launchd.agents` is a home-manager concept (user-level `~/Library/LaunchAgents`), not a system-level `/Library/LaunchDaemons`. It also only applies to macOS, so it should go under `nix/modules/darwin/` since that directory holds darwin-specific modules, but it will be a home-manager config imported via the darwin profile.

Looking at the import chain:
- `nix/profiles/darwin.nix` adds imports to `home-manager.users.yuta.imports`
- This is the correct place to add a macOS-specific home-manager module

**Script approach:** Inline via `pkgs.writeShellScript` -- the script is ~15 lines, and this keeps everything self-contained in one Nix file, consistent with how `flake.nix` already uses `writeShellScript`.

### Trigger Mechanism: WatchPaths

The launchd `WatchPaths` key will monitor `/Library/Preferences/SystemConfiguration` for any file changes. This directory gets modified whenever the network state changes (Wi-Fi connect, disconnect, DHCP lease renewal, etc.). This is the standard event-driven approach for network-change detection on macOS.

Specifically, watching the directory itself (not a single file) is more reliable because different macOS versions may update different files within it on Wi-Fi changes.

Additionally, a `ThrottleInterval` of 5 seconds prevents rapid-fire execution when multiple files change in quick succession during a single network event.

### Files to Create/Modify

#### 1. New file: `nix/modules/darwin/wallpaper-switcher.nix`

This is the complete module. It will be a home-manager module (used under `home-manager.users.yuta.imports`):

```nix
# Wi-Fi SSID に基づく壁紙自動切り替え
{ pkgs, ... }:

let
  homeWifi = "TP-Link_42B4_5G";
  homeWallpaper = "/Users/yuta/Downloads/0830/高解像度デカうい.jpg";
  defaultWallpaper = "/System/Library/Desktop Pictures/Sonoma.heic";

  wallpaper-switcher = pkgs.writeShellScript "wallpaper-switcher" ''
    sleep 2
    SSID=$(/usr/sbin/networksetup -getairportnetwork en0 2>/dev/null | /usr/bin/sed 's/Current Wi-Fi Network: //')

    if [ "$SSID" = "${homeWifi}" ]; then
      WALLPAPER="${homeWallpaper}"
    else
      WALLPAPER="${defaultWallpaper}"
    fi

    /usr/bin/osascript -e "tell application \"System Events\" to tell every desktop to set picture to \"$WALLPAPER\""
  '';
in
{
  launchd.agents.wallpaper-switcher = {
    enable = true;
    config = {
      Label = "com.yuta.wallpaper-switcher";
      ProgramArguments = [ "${wallpaper-switcher}" ];
      WatchPaths = [ "/Library/Preferences/SystemConfiguration" ];
      ThrottleInterval = 5;
      RunAtLoad = true;
      StandardOutPath = "/tmp/wallpaper-switcher.log";
      StandardErrorPath = "/tmp/wallpaper-switcher.err";
    };
  };
}
```

Key design decisions in the script:
- **`sleep 2`** at the start: When launchd triggers on a `WatchPaths` change, the network state may still be transitioning. A brief delay ensures the SSID is fully settled.
- **Full paths** (`/usr/sbin/networksetup`, `/usr/bin/sed`, `/usr/bin/osascript`): The launchd environment has a minimal `PATH`. Using absolute paths avoids breakage.
- **`RunAtLoad = true`**: Sets the correct wallpaper at login, not just on network changes.
- **`ThrottleInterval = 5`**: Prevents rapid successive executions if multiple config files change during one network event.
- **Log files**: Stored in `/tmp/` for debugging. These are ephemeral and cleared on reboot, which is appropriate for a simple wallpaper script.

#### 2. Modify: `nix/profiles/darwin.nix`

Add the wallpaper-switcher module to the home-manager imports:

```nix
{ ... }:
{
  home-manager.users.yuta.imports = [
    ./../modules/home/programs/common-cli.nix
    ./../modules/darwin/packages.nix
    ./../modules/darwin/wallpaper-switcher.nix    # <-- add this line
    ./../modules/home/programs/ghostty
  ];
}
```

### No other files need modification

The module is self-contained. No changes to `flake.nix`, `default.nix`, overlays, or any other existing files.

### Verification Steps

After running `nix run .#switch`:

1. **Check the plist was installed:**
   ```bash
   cat ~/Library/LaunchAgents/com.yuta.wallpaper-switcher.plist
   ```

2. **Check the agent is loaded:**
   ```bash
   launchctl list | grep wallpaper
   ```

3. **Check logs:**
   ```bash
   cat /tmp/wallpaper-switcher.log
   cat /tmp/wallpaper-switcher.err
   ```

4. **Test manually:**
   ```bash
   # Trigger the script directly
   launchctl kickstart gui/$(id -u)/com.yuta.wallpaper-switcher
   ```

5. **Test Wi-Fi disconnect scenario:**
   ```bash
   # Turn off Wi-Fi
   networksetup -setairportpower en0 off
   # Wait a few seconds, check wallpaper changed to Sonoma
   # Turn Wi-Fi back on
   networksetup -setairportpower en0 on
   # Wait for connection, check wallpaper changed back
   ```

### Potential Issues and Mitigations

1. **`en0` might not always be the Wi-Fi interface.** On all standard MacBooks (including M2 MacBook Air), `en0` is Wi-Fi. This is safe to hardcode. If it ever changed, the `networksetup` command would return an error, and the script would fall through to the default wallpaper -- a safe fallback.

2. **Wallpaper path with Japanese characters.** The `osascript` command handles Unicode paths correctly on macOS. We already confirmed this works -- the current wallpaper is set to the exact same Japanese-named file.

3. **home-manager launchd agent lifecycle.** When you run `nix run .#switch`, home-manager will automatically:
   - Write the plist to `~/Library/LaunchAgents/com.yuta.wallpaper-switcher.plist`
   - Run `launchctl load` to register the agent
   - On subsequent rebuilds, if the plist changed, it will `unload` then `load` again

4. **System Events permission.** The `osascript` command to set wallpaper may require the terminal/script to have "Automation" permissions in System Preferences > Privacy & Security. Since this is run from launchd (not a terminal), macOS should prompt for permission on first run. If it does not work silently, you may need to grant permissions manually in System Preferences.

### Critical Files for Implementation

- `/Users/yuta/ghq/github.com/yutakobayashidev/dotnix/nix/profiles/darwin.nix` - Add the import for the new wallpaper-switcher module
- `/Users/yuta/ghq/github.com/yutakobayashidev/dotnix/nix/modules/darwin/packages.nix` - Reference for the module pattern (home-manager module under darwin/)
- `/Users/yuta/ghq/github.com/yutakobayashidev/dotnix/nix/modules/home/dotfiles.nix` - Reference for launchctl usage pattern already in the codebase
- `/Users/yuta/ghq/github.com/yutakobayashidev/dotnix/flake.nix` - Reference for writeShellScript pattern
- `/Users/yuta/ghq/github.com/yutakobayashidev/dotnix/nix/hosts/M2-MacBook-Air/default.nix` - Host config to understand the full import chain

  "old_string": "# Wi-Fi SSID 管理: sops-nix + NetworkManager ensureProfiles\n\n## Context\n\nUM790-Pro (NixOS) の Wi-Fi SSID を Nix で宣言的に管理したい。PSK は sops-nix で暗号化してリポジトリにコミットする。将来的に macOS での壁紙自動切替の基盤にもなる。\n\n## 変更ファイル一覧\n\n| Action | File | 内容 |\n|--------|------|------|\n| Modify | `flake.nix` | `sops-nix` input 追加 |\n| Modify | `nix/hosts/UM790-Pro/default.nix` | `inputs.sops-nix.nixosModules.sops` を modules に追加 |\n| Modify | `nix/profiles/gui.nix` | `sops.nix` と `networking.nix` を import |\n| Create | `nix/modules/linux/sops.nix` | sops-nix 設定（age key, secrets 宣言） |\n| Create | `nix/modules/linux/networking.nix` | NM ensureProfiles で Wi-Fi SSID 定義 |\n| Create | `.sops.yaml` | sops 暗号化ルール（age public key） |\n| Create | `secrets/secrets.yaml` | 暗号化された Wi-Fi パスワード |\n\n## 実装手順\n\n### 1. flake.nix に sops-nix 追加\n\n```nix\nsops-nix = {\n  url = \"github:Mic92/sops-nix\";\n  inputs.nixpkgs.follows = \"nixpkgs\";\n};\n```\n\n### 2. UM790-Pro host に sops-nix NixOS module をロード\n\n`nix/hosts/UM790-Pro/default.nix` の modules に追加:\n```nix\ninputs.sops-nix.nixosModules.sops\n```\n\n### 3. `nix/modules/linux/sops.nix` を作成\n\n```nix\n{ ... }:\n{\n  sops = {\n    defaultSopsFile = ../../../secrets/secrets.yaml;\n    age.sshKeyPaths = [ \"/etc/ssh/ssh_host_ed25519_key\" ];\n    secrets.wifi_env = {\n      mode = \"0400\";\n      restartUnits = [ \"NetworkManager.service\" ];\n    };\n  };\n}\n```\n\n- SSH ホスト鍵から age 鍵を導出（追加の鍵生成不要）\n- `wifi_env` は `/run/secrets/wifi_env` に復号される\n\n### 4. `nix/modules/linux/networking.nix` を作成\n\n```nix\n{ config, ... }:\n{\n  networking.networkmanager.ensureProfiles = {\n    environmentFiles = [ config.sops.secrets.wifi_env.path ];\n    profiles = {\n      home-wifi = {\n        connection = {\n          id = \"HOME_SSID\";  # 実際の SSID に置換\n          type = \"wifi\";\n          autoconnect = true;\n        };\n        wifi = {\n          mode = \"infrastructure\";\n          ssid = \"HOME_SSID\";  # 実際の SSID に置換\n        };\n        wifi-security = {\n          key-mgmt = \"wpa-psk\";\n          psk = \"$WIFI_PSK_HOME\";  # environmentFiles から展開\n        };\n        ipv4.method = \"auto\";\n        ipv6 = {\n          method = \"auto\";\n          addr-gen-mode = \"default\";\n        };\n      };\n    };\n  };\n}\n```\n\n### 5. `nix/profiles/gui.nix` に import 追加\n\n```nix\nimports = [\n  ...\n  ./../modules/linux/sops.nix\n  ./../modules/linux/networking.nix\n];\n```\n\ngui.nix に置く理由: WSL (Still-Legend-x870) は Wi-Fi 不要かつ sops の SSH ホスト鍵がない。GUI ホスト = 物理マシン = Wi-Fi が必要。\n\n### 6. `.sops.yaml` と `secrets/secrets.yaml` を作成\n\nUM790-Pro 上で実行:\n```bash\nnix-shell -p sops ssh-to-age\n\n# age public key を取得\ncat /etc/ssh/ssh_host_ed25519_key.pub | ssh-to-age\n# → .sops.yaml に貼り付け\n\n# secrets を作成・暗号化\nmkdir -p secrets\nsops secrets/secrets.yaml\n# エディタで入力:\n#   wifi_env: |\n#     WIFI_PSK_HOME=実際のパスワード\n```\n\n## 検証\n\n```bash\n# 1. ビルド確認\nnix run .#build\n\n# 2. 適用\nnix run .#switch\n\n# 3. secrets 復号確認\nsudo cat /run/secrets/wifi_env\n\n# 4. NM プロファイル確認\nnmcli connection show\n```",
  "new_string": "# macOS Wi-Fi SSID ベース壁紙自動切替\n\n## Context\n\n自宅 Wi-Fi (`TP-Link_42B4_5G`) への接続状態に応じて macOS の壁紙を自動切替する。自宅ではオタク壁紙、外出時はデフォルト壁紙に。\n\n## 変更ファイル一覧\n\n| Action | File | 内容 |\n|--------|------|------|\n| Create | `nix/modules/darwin/wallpaper-switcher.nix` | home-manager launchd agent + スクリプト |\n| Modify | `nix/profiles/darwin.nix` | wallpaper-switcher.nix を import |\n\n## 実装\n\n### 1. `nix/modules/darwin/wallpaper-switcher.nix` を作成\n\nhome-manager モジュールとして `launchd.agents` を定義:\n\n- **スクリプト**: `networksetup -getairportnetwork en0` で SSID 取得 → `osascript` で壁紙設定\n- **トリガー**: `WatchPaths = [ \"/Library/Preferences/SystemConfiguration\" ]`（ネットワーク変更でイベント発火）\n- **`RunAtLoad = true`**: ログイン時にも実行\n- **`ThrottleInterval = 5`**: 連続発火を抑制\n- **`sleep 2`**: ネットワーク遷移が安定するまで待機\n- コマンドはすべてフルパス（launchd 環境は PATH が最小限）\n\n壁紙:\n- 自宅: `/Users/yuta/Downloads/0830/高解像度デカうい.jpg`\n- 外出: `/System/Library/Desktop Pictures/Sonoma.heic`\n\n### 2. `nix/profiles/darwin.nix` に import 追加\n\n```nix\nhome-manager.users.yuta.imports = [\n  ./../modules/home/programs/common-cli.nix\n  ./../modules/darwin/packages.nix\n  ./../modules/darwin/wallpaper-switcher.nix  # 追加\n  ./../modules/home/programs/ghostty\n];\n```\n\n## 検証\n\n```bash\n# 適用\nnix run .#switch\n\n# plist 確認\ncat ~/Library/LaunchAgents/com.yuta.wallpaper-switcher.plist\n\n# agent 動作確認\nlaunchctl list | grep wallpaper\n\n# ログ確認\ncat /tmp/wallpaper-switcher.log\n\n# 手動トリガー\nlaunchctl kickstart gui/$(id -u)/com.yuta.wallpaper-switcher\n```"
}

## Approved Plan:
# macOS Wi-Fi SSID ベース壁紙自動切替

## Context

自宅 Wi-Fi (`TP-Link_42B4_5G`) への接続状態に応じて macOS の壁紙を自動切替する。自宅ではオタク壁紙、外出時はデフォルト壁紙に。

## 変更ファイル一覧

| Action | File | 内容 |
|--------|------|------|
| Create | `nix/modules/darwin/wallpaper-switcher.nix` | home-manager launchd agent + スクリプト |
| Modify | `nix/profiles/darwin.nix` | wallpaper-switcher.nix を import |

## 実装

### 1. `nix/modules/darwin/wallpaper-switcher.nix` を作成

home-manager モジュールとして `launchd.agents` を定義:

- **スクリプト**: `networksetup -getairportnetwork en0` で SSID 取得 → `osascript` で壁紙設定
- **トリガー**: `WatchPaths = [ "/Library/Preferences/SystemConfiguration" ]`（ネットワーク変更でイベント発火）
- **`RunAtLoad = true`**: ログイン時にも実行
- **`ThrottleInterval = 5`**: 連続発火を抑制
- **`sleep 2`**: ネットワーク遷移が安定するまで待機
- コマンドはすべてフルパス（launchd 環境は PATH が最小限）

壁紙:
- 自宅: `/Users/yuta/Downloads/0830/高解像度デカうい.jpg`
- 外出: `/System/Library/Desktop Pictures/Sonoma.heic`

### 2. `nix/profiles/darwin.nix` に import 追加

```nix
home-manager.users.yuta.imports = [
  ./../modules/home/programs/common-cli.nix
  ./../modules/darwin/packages.nix
  ./../modules/darwin/wallpaper-switcher.nix  # 追加
  ./../modules/home/programs/ghostty
];
```

## 検証

```bash
# 適用
nix run .#switch

# plist 確認
cat ~/Library/LaunchAgents/com.yuta.wallpaper-switcher.plist

# agent 動作確認
launchctl list | grep wallpaper

# ログ確認
cat /tmp/wallpaper-switcher.log

# 手動トリガー
launchctl kickstart gui/$(id -u)/com.yuta.wallpaper-switcher
```

```

id: toolu_01DesoZyUhX1nPkXPnx9q7jC
```json
{
  "file_path": "/Users/yuta/ghq/github.com/yutakobayashidev/dotnix/nix/modules/darwin/wallpaper-switcher.nix",
  "content": "# Wi-Fi SSID に基づく壁紙自動切り替え\n{ pkgs, ... }:\n\nlet\n  homeWifi = \"TP-Link_42B4_5G\";\n  homeWallpaper = \"/Users/yuta/Downloads/0830/高解像度デカうい.jpg\";\n  defaultWallpaper = \"/System/Library/Desktop Pictures/Sonoma.heic\";\n\n  wallpaper-switcher = pkgs.writeShellScript \"wallpaper-switcher\" ''\n    sleep 2\n    SSID=$(/usr/sbin/networksetup -getairportnetwork en0 2>/dev/null | /usr/bin/sed 's/Current Wi-Fi Network: //')\n\n    if [ \"$SSID\" = \"${homeWifi}\" ]; then\n      WALLPAPER=\"${homeWallpaper}\"\n    else\n      WALLPAPER=\"${defaultWallpaper}\"\n    fi\n\n    /usr/bin/osascript -e \"tell application \\\"System Events\\\" to tell every desktop to set picture to \\\"$WALLPAPER\\\"\"\n  '';\nin\n{\n  launchd.agents.wallpaper-switcher = {\n    enable = true;\n    config = {\n      Label = \"com.yuta.wallpaper-switcher\";\n      ProgramArguments = [ \"${wallpaper-switcher}\" ];\n      WatchPaths = [ \"/Library/Preferences/SystemConfiguration\" ];\n      ThrottleInterval = 5;\n      RunAtLoad = true;\n      StandardOutPath = \"/tmp/wallpaper-switcher.log\";\n      StandardErrorPath = \"/tmp/wallpaper-switcher.err\";\n    };\n  };\n}\n"
}
```

---

`settings.json` のコンフリクトについてですが、home-manager が管理する `settings.json`（`claude-code.nix` で生成）と、既存の手書き `settings.json` が衝突しています。home-manager はシンボリックリンクで管理するので、既にファイルが存在すると上書きを拒否します。

> TOOL

tool_use Bash
id: toolu_01AgWzEy9rrecy7UUo9RY7G3
```json
{
  "command": "ls -la /Users/yuta/.config/claude/settings.json",
  "description": "Check if settings.json is now a symlink"
}
```

> TOOL

tool_result
id: toolu_01AgWzEy9rrecy7UUo9RY7G3
```
lrwxr-xr-x yuta staff 91 B Fri Feb 27 12:13:27 2026 /Users/yuta/.config/claude/settings.json ⇒ /nix/store/REDACTED/.config/claude/settings.json
```

> AGENT

今はシンボリックリンクになっています。バックアップした `.bak` はもう不要なので消して大丈夫です。

原因は恐らくこのマシンで `nix run .#switch` が久しぶりだったか、以前 Claude Code が直接 `settings.json` を書き換えた（シンボリンクを実ファイルに置き換えた）ためです。今後もし再発する場合は `home-manager.backupFileExtension` を設定するか、`force = true` を `claude-code.nix` 側に入れると回避できます。

> AGENT

見つかりました。静止画なら `TahoeLight.heic` が使えます。

> TOOL

tool_use Read
id: toolu_015UhFsa1cSFG2V7MYdExnMu
```json
{
  "file_path": "/Users/yuta/ghq/github.com/yutakobayashidev/dotnix/nix/modules/darwin/wallpaper-switcher.nix"
}
```

> TOOL

tool_result
id: toolu_015UhFsa1cSFG2V7MYdExnMu
```
     1→# Wi-Fi SSID に基づく壁紙自動切り替え
     2→{ pkgs, ... }:
     3→
     4→let
     5→  homeWifi = "TP-Link_42B4_5G";
     6→  homeWallpaper = "/Users/yuta/Downloads/0830/高解像度デカうい.jpg";
     7→  defaultWallpaper = "/System/Library/Desktop Pictures/Sonoma.heic";
     8→
     9→  wallpaper-switcher = pkgs.writeShellScript "wallpaper-switcher" ''
    10→    sleep 2
    11→    SSID=$(/usr/sbin/networksetup -getairportnetwork en0 2>/dev/null | /usr/bin/sed 's/Current Wi-Fi Network: //')
    12→
    13→    if [ "$SSID" = "${homeWifi}" ]; then
    14→      WALLPAPER="${homeWallpaper}"
    15→    else
    16→      WALLPAPER="${defaultWallpaper}"
    17→    fi
    18→
    19→    /usr/bin/osascript -e "tell application \"System Events\" to tell every desktop to set picture to \"$WALLPAPER\""
    20→  '';
    21→in
    22→{
    23→  launchd.agents.wallpaper-switcher = {
    24→    enable = true;
    25→    config = {
    26→      Label = "com.yuta.wallpaper-switcher";
    27→      ProgramArguments = [ "${wallpaper-switcher}" ];
    28→      WatchPaths = [ "/Library/Preferences/SystemConfiguration" ];
    29→      ThrottleInterval = 5;
    30→      RunAtLoad = true;
    31→      StandardOutPath = "/tmp/wallpaper-switcher.log";
    32→      StandardErrorPath = "/tmp/wallpaper-switcher.err";
    33→    };
    34→  };
    35→}
    36→
```

> TOOL

tool_use Edit
id: toolu_01FKN2d55TcXgxSDWS8BREs8
```json
{
  "replace_all": false,
  "file_path": "/Users/yuta/ghq/github.com/yutakobayashidev/dotnix/nix/modules/darwin/wallpaper-switcher.nix",
  "old_string": "  defaultWallpaper = \"/System/Library/Desktop Pictures/Sonoma.heic\";",
  "new_string": "  defaultWallpaper = \"/System/Library/ExtensionKit/Extensions/NeptuneOneWallpaper.appex/Contents/Resources/TahoeLight.heic\";"
}
```

I’m trying to retrieve the name of my Wi-Fi network via the terminal, but I’m encountering some issues.

I tried with the command:

/System/Library/PrivateFrameworks/Apple80211.framework/Versions/Current/Resources/airport -s
However, it returns the following warning:

WARNING: The airport command line tool is deprecated and will be removed in a future release.
For diagnosing Wi-Fi related issues, use the Wireless Diagnostics app or the wdutil command line tool.
I also tried:

networksetup -getairportnetwork en0
But it returns:

You are not associated with an AirPort network.
Even though I am connected to Wi-Fi. I have tried disconnecting and reconnecting, as well as deleting the following .plist files in /Library/Preferences/SystemConfiguration/:

com.apple.airport.preferences.plist
com.apple.network.identification.plist
com.apple.wifi.message-tracer.plist
NetworkInterfaces.plist
preferences.plist
Despite these efforts, I still have not resolved the issue.

I DON'T want to use wdutil since it requires the sudo password. Is there any way to fix the problem with networksetup, or is there an alternative method to retrieve the Wi-Fi name?

Specifications: macOS Sequoia 15.0 Apple Silicon M3 Pro

UPDATE: Today I reset my mac to the factory settings and the system installed Sonoma as the default OS. I have tried the terminal command networksetup and it works fine. After updating again to Sequoia the problem appears again so I think the problem is directly related to this new update. Unfortunately, I can't find any info about deprecation or warnings on the man.

macosnetworkcommand-linewifiairport

11

From my limited testing networksetup -listpreferredwirelessnetworks en0 outputs a list of Wifi SSIDs where the first one is the one you're connected to.

Then it would be a matter of formatting the output:

networksetup -listpreferredwirelessnetworks en0 | sed -n '2 p' | tr -d '\t'
I'd like to confirm if this behavior is the same for others as well.

EDIT:

Also works:

ipconfig getsummary en0 | awk -F ' SSID : '  '/ SSID : / {print $2}'
Credit: https://github.com/FelixKratz/SketchyBar/issues/616#issuecomment-2370891970

try _get_wifi_ifname() {
  if ! scutil <<< list |
    awk -F/ '/Setup:.*AirPort$/{i=$(NF-1);exit} END {if(i) {print i} else {exit 1}}'; then
    scutil <<< list | awk -F/ '/en[0-9]+\/AirPort$/ {print $(NF-1);exit}'
  fi
}

networksetup -listpreferredwirelessnetworks "$(_get_wifi_ifname)" |
awk 'NR==2 && sub("\t","") { print; exit }'

     1→# Lessons
     2→
     3→## 2026-02-26: gh-poi が git worktree 対応済み → git-wt-clean は不要
     4→
     5→- `gh-poi` はマージ済みブランチの worktree 自動削除、locked worktree スキップ、linked worktree 上での実行をサポート済み
     6→- `git-wt-clean` は `git branch -vv` の `gone` フラグ（追跡先リモートブランチが削除済み）に依存していたが、これは upstream 未設定のブランチを検出できず、削除理由も区別できない
     7→- `gh-poi` は GitHub API で PR 状態を直接確認するため、より正確で安全
     8→- 自前スクリプトを書く前に、既存ツールの最新機能を確認すること

> AGENT

今は自宅 Wi-Fi に繋がっているので、オタク壁紙になっているはずです。

外出をシミュレートするには Wi-Fi を切断します：

```bash
networksetup -setairportpower en0 off
```

数秒後に Tahoe に切り替わるはず。戻すには：

```bash

> AGENT

`com.apple.airport.preferences.plist` が 12:26 に更新されています。Wi-Fi のオフにしたときのはずですが、launchd がトリガーされなかった。macOS Tahoe では `WatchPaths` のディレクトリ監視が効かなくなっている可能性があります。

`StartInterval` でのポーリングに切り替えます。30秒間隔で十分軽量です。

     1→# Wi-Fi SSID に基づく壁紙自動切り替え
     2→{ pkgs, ... }:
     3→
     4→let
     5→  homeWifi = "TP-Link_42B4_5G";
     6→  homeWallpaper = "/Users/yuta/Downloads/0830/高解像度デカうい.jpg";
     7→  defaultWallpaper = "/System/Library/ExtensionKit/Extensions/NeptuneOneWallpaper.appex/Contents/Resources/TahoeLight.heic";
     8→
     9→  wallpaper-switcher = pkgs.writeShellScript "wallpaper-switcher" ''
    10→    sleep 2
    11→    WIFI_IF=$(/usr/sbin/scutil <<< list | /usr/bin/awk -F/ '/Setup:.*AirPort$/{i=$(NF-1);exit} END {if(i) {print i} else {exit 1}}') ||
    12→      WIFI_IF=$(/usr/sbin/scutil <<< list | /usr/bin/awk -F/ '/en[0-9]+\/AirPort$/ {print $(NF-1);exit}')
    13→    SSID=$(/usr/sbin/networksetup -listpreferredwirelessnetworks "$WIFI_IF" 2>/dev/null | /usr/bin/awk 'NR==2 && sub("\t","") { print; exit }')
    14→
    15→    if [ "$SSID" = "${homeWifi}" ]; then
    16→      WALLPAPER="${homeWallpaper}"
    17→    else
    18→      WALLPAPER="${defaultWallpaper}"
    19→    fi
    20→
    21→    /usr/bin/osascript -e "tell application \"System Events\" to tell every desktop to set picture to \"$WALLPAPER\""
    22→  '';
    23→in
    24→{
    25→  launchd.agents.wallpaper-switcher = {
    26→    enable = true;
    27→    config = {
    28→      Label = "com.yuta.wallpaper-switcher";
    29→      ProgramArguments = [ "${wallpaper-switcher}" ];
    30→      WatchPaths = [ "/Library/Preferences/SystemConfiguration" ];
    31→      ThrottleInterval = 5;

```
#!/nix/store/REDACTED.3p9/bin/bash
sleep 2
WIFI_IF=$(/usr/sbin/scutil <<< list | /usr/bin/awk -F/ '/Setup:.*AirPort$/{i=$(NF-1);exit} END {if(i) {print i} else {exit 1}}') ||
  WIFI_IF=$(/usr/sbin/scutil <<< list | /usr/bin/awk -F/ '/en[0-9]+\/AirPort$/ {print $(NF-1);exit}')
SSID=$(/usr/sbin/networksetup -listpreferredwirelessnetworks "$WIFI_IF" 2>/dev/null | /usr/bin/awk 'NR==2 && sub("\t","") { print; exit }')

if [ "$SSID" = "TP-Link_42B4_5G" ]; then
  WALLPAPER="/Users/yuta/Downloads/0830/高解像度デカうい.jpg"
else
  WALLPAPER="/System/Library/ExtensionKit/Extensions/NeptuneOneWallpaper.appex/Contents/Resources/TahoeLight.heic"
fi

/usr/bin/osascript -e "tell application \"System Events\" to tell every desktop to set picture to \"$WALLPAPER\""
```

id: toolu_01JKjavVuG2WGWWcbTT9cYUT
```
TP-Link_42B4_5G
```

> AGENT

原因判明。`listpreferredwirelessnetworks` は **保存済みネットワークの優先順リスト** なので、Wi-Fi オフでも常に同じ結果を返します。接続状態を反映しません。

別のアプローチが必要です。Wi-Fi の電源状態を先にチェックします。

> AGENT

`SSID_STR` が空です。scutil の State でも SSID が取れません。`listpreferredwirelessnetworks` は保存済みリストなので接続状態を反映しない。

Wi-Fi の電源状態で判定するアプローチに切り替えます。

> TOOL

tool_use Bash
id: toolu_0142D36Vcd8DDDQuvn7TTdWi
```json
{
  "command": "/usr/sbin/networksetup -getairportpower en0",
  "description": "Check Wi-Fi power state"
}
```

> TOOL

tool_result
id: toolu_0142D36Vcd8DDDQuvn7TTdWi
```
Wi-Fi Power (en0): On
```

> TOOL

tool_use Bash
id: toolu_01YNkiDRZ2ttFupSjfKNoAZ4
```json
{
  "command": "route -n get default 2>/dev/null | grep gateway",
  "description": "Check default gateway IP"
}
```

> TOOL

tool_result
id: toolu_01YNkiDRZ2ttFupSjfKNoAZ4
```
    gateway: 192.168.0.1
```

> AGENT

SSID が全部 redacted されるので、別の方法で自宅ネットワークを判定しましょう。**デフォルトゲートウェイの IP**（`192.168.0.1`）はルーター固有なので使えます。

> TOOL

tool_use Edit
id: toolu_01Cpp9VzprMBwD5vrC4Hsz1T
```json
{
  "replace_all": false,
  "file_path": "/Users/yuta/ghq/github.com/yutakobayashidev/dotnix/nix/modules/darwin/wallpaper-switcher.nix",
  "old_string": "  homeWifi = \"TP-Link_42B4_5G\";\n  homeWallpaper = \"/Users/yuta/Downloads/0830/高解像度デカうい.jpg\";\n  defaultWallpaper = \"/System/Library/ExtensionKit/Extensions/NeptuneOneWallpaper.appex/Contents/Resources/TahoeLight.heic\";\n\n  wallpaper-switcher = pkgs.writeShellScript \"wallpaper-switcher\" ''\n    WIFI_IF=$(/usr/sbin/scutil <<< list | /usr/bin/awk -F/ '/Setup:.*AirPort$/{i=$(NF-1);exit} END {if(i) {print i} else {exit 1}}') ||\n      WIFI_IF=$(/usr/sbin/scutil <<< list | /usr/bin/awk -F/ '/en[0-9]+\\/AirPort$/ {print $(NF-1);exit}')\n    SSID=$(/usr/sbin/networksetup -listpreferredwirelessnetworks \"$WIFI_IF\" 2>/dev/null | /usr/bin/awk 'NR==2 && sub(\"\\t\",\"\") { print; exit }')\n\n    if [ \"$SSID\" = \"${homeWifi}\" ]; then\n      WALLPAPER=\"${homeWallpaper}\"\n    else\n      WALLPAPER=\"${defaultWallpaper}\"\n    fi\n\n    /usr/bin/osascript -e \"tell application \\\"System Events\\\" to tell every desktop to set picture to \\\"$WALLPAPER\\\"\"\n  '';",
  "new_string": "  homeGateway = \"192.168.0.1\";\n  homeWallpaper = \"/Users/yuta/Downloads/0830/高解像度デカうい.jpg\";\n  defaultWallpaper = \"/System/Library/ExtensionKit/Extensions/NeptuneOneWallpaper.appex/Contents/Resources/TahoeLight.heic\";\n\n  wallpaper-switcher = pkgs.writeShellScript \"wallpaper-switcher\" ''\n    GATEWAY=$(/sbin/route -n get default 2>/dev/null | /usr/bin/awk '/gateway:/{print $2}')\n\n    if [ \"$GATEWAY\" = \"${homeGateway}\" ]; then\n      WALLPAPER=\"${homeWallpaper}\"\n    else\n      WALLPAPER=\"${defaultWallpaper}\"\n    fi\n\n    /usr/bin/osascript -e \"tell application \\\"System Events\\\" to tell every desktop to set picture to \\\"$WALLPAPER\\\"\"\n  '';"
}
```
